# TPC source-Markdown conversion coverage: 418–140

Updated 2026-09-10. 279 preserved manuscripts have source-complete mechanical Markdown. The repair covered the prior 64 conversions (TPC355–418) plus TPC350–354; subsequent existing-source batches are included in the links below. This record supersedes older inconsistent counts and generic review claims; it creates no paper or route edge.

## Method and explicit limits

The read-only [converter](../../90_%E9%99%84%E4%BB%B6/%E5%8E%9F%E5%A7%8B%E8%B5%84%E6%96%99/research/tpc-big-road/maintain_source_markdown.py.txt) uses the installed Pandoc LaTeX reader and math-enabled Markdown writer. Full abstract/body formula sequences and normalized plain text are compared after Markdown parsing. Each conversion record locks the source commit and TeX/PDF hashes, catalogues raw displayed equations, and maps source section lines to actual extracted PDF heading matches. Missing or multiple page hits remain explicit. No PDF is recompiled or claimed to be proven synchronized with its TeX.

Both preserved manuscript layouts are supported: paper/main.tex and root main.tex, with exactly one versioned source and no competing local layout. All reading layers remain at paper/main.md; root-source links point back to the originals rather than copying or moving them. Root-layout PDFs are selected from the exact directory-basename PDF, main.pdf, or paper.pdf, in that order, and must be versioned. Duplicate paper numbers require an explicit existing directory basename; a missing sibling is never replaced by another manuscript’s conversion.

For the reviewed multi-file manuscripts, literal local inputs are expanded in memory with an ordered dependency ledger, commit-locked original bytes, and original file/line locations. Besides standalone inputs, two narrowly defined root-source command-only compact forms are supported: adjacent inputs and exact abstract wrappers, with or without the combined begin-document/maketitle prefix. Unsupported control-flow contexts, rebound/symlinked paths, and unsupported line separators fail closed. Explicit CR/CRLF normalization changes only reader whitespace, records separator counts, retains original LF-delimited source line locations and byte hashes, and never repairs suspected source commands. It is not a TeX interpreter: future source forms require their own review. The separate system glyph-map exception remains hash-allowlisted, preamble-only, and non-executing.

Every detected source bibliography is retained, including the complete external BibTeX files when present. Per-paper records identify and hash-lock these dependencies; sources without references are not supplied with invented entries. Theorem/proof names and boundaries remain; printed numbering and unresolved citation formatting are not reconstructed. Where a separate proof package is absent, only available original notes are linked below; neither their existence nor their filenames supply a proof-review receipt.

The [repair and bounded manual audit](TPC_MAINTENANCE_REPAIR_2026-09-07--4ef5847e.md) documents the original text-trimming defect, corrected mapping evidence, per-paper TPC350–354 prerequisite checks, the unresolved TPC352 manuscript/ producer operator mismatch, TPC353–354 notation issues, and the TPC402 ambiguous page match. Automated preservation is not theorem validation. Numerical certificates and the production cascade were not rerun.

## Coverage and next work

Across 420 entries: `full-source-md=279`, `reliable-full-md=0`, `partial-or-notes=140`, `source-inaccessible=1`. The 140 partial/notes entries remain the accessible conversion pool; the source-inaccessible entry is specifically the moving-hole translation- compiler directory for TPC207, not the separate critical-moving-hole paper. Continue from the inventory, preserving original sources and hand edits and recording unsupported conversions rather than guessing. This is not a claim that all three archive-maintenance tasks are finished.

The [remaining checklist](PAPER_MATERIALS_REMAINING--49b33b63.md) enumerates all existing directories and separates conversion from reliable semantic review. The [current handoff](../../05_%E7%BB%BC%E5%90%88%E4%B8%8E%E8%BF%9B%E5%B1%95/%E4%BA%A4%E6%8E%A5%E6%96%87%E6%A1%A3-%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E5%BF%AB%E7%85%A7.md) records the three original maintenance tasks, the completed work reused, and the remaining source/review limitations.

## Scientific stop remains unchanged

`TPC418_ROUND2_CLUE = NONE_UNTIL_GROWING_OR_PHYSICAL_GATE_CHANGES`. Arithmetic advance remains `NO`, fixed-power credit remains `0`, and the full gate remains `OPEN`. No TPC419 or new theorem is created.
